{"forms":[{"form":"preterite-present verbs","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A type of verb specific to Germanic languages in which the forms of the present tense resemble the forms normally found in the preterite (or past) tense of strong verbs.
